# M3 Ask Ottie Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

Status: Proposed (plan only, 2026-10-21). Task 2.13 in `docs/TASKS.md`. Branch at build time: `feat/m3-ask-ottie`.

**Goal:** Ask Ottie, a small and well-evaluated RAG system on dins.dev: a Python build that turns the approved library and published pages into one binary index, a Worker endpoint that searches it and streams short, cited answers from Claude Haiku inside a hard daily budget, a Svelte island that shows the answer and how it was made, and evals that hold it to the PRD bars.

**Architecture:** `rag/` (Python, uv) chunks Markdown and MDX by heading, embeds chunks with the open bge-m3 weights on Din's machine, quantizes them to int8 and writes `site/public/ask/index-v1.bin` together with a BM25 keyword index in a documented little-endian layout. The built index is committed, and CI only checks that it is fresh (no model download in CI). The Worker reads the index through the `ASSETS` binding into typed-array views once per isolate, embeds the question with bge-m3 on Workers AI, runs hybrid search (cosine plus BM25, fused by reciprocal rank), and takes a canned path (don't know, declined, redirect, resting) without calling the model whenever it can. Otherwise it reserves budget in D1 with one atomic statement, streams from an `AnswerProvider` (a fake or Anthropic) as NDJSON, settles the real cost and writes the anonymous log. The island renders plain text through a tiny formatter that never produces HTML.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.12 with uv, pydantic 2.14, PyYAML 6.0, numpy 2.5, sentence-transformers 6.1 (optional `embed` extra), anthropic 1.17 (optional `evals` extra), pytest, Ruff. TypeScript 6.0, Astro 7.4 with @astrojs/cloudflare 14.4, Svelte 5.57, Vitest 4.1 with the Cloudflare Workers integration, Playwright 1.64 with @axe-core/playwright, @anthropic-ai/sdk 0.137, tsx 4.23 and yaml 2.x for scripts, wrangler 4.151, D1, Workers AI (`@cf/baai/bge-m3`), Turnstile, the Workers rate limiting binding, Cron Triggers.

**Spec:** `docs/site/PRD.md` sections 2 (library), 3 (Ask Ottie) and 6 (quality); `docs/site/PLAN.md` M3; decisions in `docs/WORK_LEDGER.md` (2026-10-21 entries); voice in `docs/BRAND.md`.

## Global Constraints

Every task's requirements include these lines.

- Answers capped at about 350 tokens (hard `max_tokens: 400`, the prompt asks for at most 120 words); questions at most 300 characters; the last 3 turns kept.
- Daily budget about $1 in the Worker (`ASK_DAILY_BUDGET_USD`, default `"1"`), enforced atomically in D1 before any model call; a $100 hard limit on the Anthropic production workspace and about $5 on the eval workspace (set by Din, see M5).
- Retrieval returns the top 6 chunks, filtered to one project on project pages.
- If nothing scores high enough, Ottie answers "I don't know that one. Din would, here's how to ask." **without calling the model**. Ottie never guesses.
- No tools and no side effects in the model call. Answers render as plain text with simple formatting, never raw HTML (no `{@html}` anywhere in Ask Ottie).
- Resting mode when the API is down or the budget is spent: best-matching pages as links plus Din's contact links. Keyword-only search when Workers AI is down.
- Anonymous question log: question, sources used, scores, thumbs up or down, no IP address, no cookies, kept 30 days, deleted by a nightly cron.
- Site builds never call the model. Suggested answers are generated by a script, reviewed by Din in a PR and served as static text.
- Two answer backends behind one interface: the fake for tests, local work and preview deploys; Anthropic for live checks and production.
- Free Workers plan: about 10 ms CPU per request, 50 subrequests, so no JSON parsing of vectors at request time.
- CI fails on an unapproved library entry or a deny-list topic (visa status, finances, personal life). Only Din sets `approved_on`.
- Eval bars (starting, revisit after the first baseline): recall@6 at least 0.9, faithfulness at least 0.95, 100 percent correct refusals on the deny-list, answers in the question's language. Retrieval evals on every PR; answer evals on manual trigger with the eval key; Haiku judges routine runs, Sonnet judges release runs.
- Ottie talks about Din, never as Din; English, and French when asked in French. Refer to Din by name, never with pronouns.
- Sample library entries are clearly marked samples (`sample: true`, topic starting "Sample:") and state only true facts about the site, never invented facts about Din.
- Writing style everywhere (code comments, UI strings, docs, commits): no em dashes, no en dashes as separators, no AI buzzwords. Commits use Conventional Commits with no attribution lines.
- Pin dependencies to minor versions. Stop and ask Din before downloading the bge-m3 weights (about 2.3 GB), before any step that spends API money, before creating Cloudflare resources, pushing, opening PRs or deploying.

## Review Focus

Inputs the spec implies but that are easy to miss. Each has a test in the task named.

1. **Short follow-up questions** ("and what stack?") after a first question: retrieval would miss without context. Expected: the previous question is added to the retrieval query. Test: Task 25 (`pipeline.test.ts`, "follow-up uses the previous question").
2. **A visitor closing the tab mid-answer:** the stream aborts. Expected: the reservation stays charged (never under-counted), the log row is still written, nothing throws. Test: Task 27 ("client disconnect keeps the reservation").
3. **Two tabs at the budget edge:** concurrent questions when one reservation is left. Expected: exactly the affordable number reserve, the rest rest. Test: Task 15 ("concurrent reservations never overshoot").
4. **Model output with HTML, Markdown links or a fake citation:** `<script>`, `[click](https://evil)`, `[9]` with 6 sources. Expected: shown as literal text, no external link, no chip for `[9]`. Test: Task 29 (`format.test.ts`).
5. **Questions that tokenize to nothing or to accents only:** emoji, "?", "Qu'est-ce que c'est ?". Expected: keyword search returns no hits and the don't-know path runs; French accents tokenize the same in Python and TypeScript. Tests: Task 4 (shared tokenizer fixture), Task 19 (`search.test.ts`, "empty query").

site/

  src/content/library/sample-*.md    sample entries about the site

Shared data files live where the runtime needs them and Python reads them from there: `site/src/lib/ask/stopwords.json` and `site/src/lib/ask/denylist.json`.

## Key interfaces (for implementers who see one task)

Python (`ottie_rag`):

- `load_settings(path: Path | None = None) -> Settings`
- `Document(source, url, kind, title, project, language, body, sample, approved_on)`
- `Chunk(id, source, url, kind, project, language, title, heading, text, sample)`
- `tokenize(text: str, keep_stopwords: bool = False) -> list[str]`, `fnv1a32(s: str) -> int`, `question_key(q: str) -> bytes`
- `Embedder` protocol: `id: str`, `dim: int`, `embed(texts: list[str]) -> np.ndarray` (float32, L2-normalized rows)
- `write_index(...) -> bytes`, `read_index(data: bytes) -> IndexData`, `write_query_cache(...) -> bytes`, `read_query_cache(data: bytes) -> QueryCache`
- `search(index: IndexData, qvec: np.ndarray | None, question: str, project: str | None = None, top_k: int = 6) -> SearchResult`

TypeScript (`site/src/lib/ask/`):

- `parseIndex(buf: ArrayBuffer): AskIndex`
- `searchIndex(ix: AskIndex, qvec: Float32Array | null, question: string, project: string | null, topK?: number): SearchResult`
- `planAnswer(input: PlanInput): AskPlan`
- `interface AnswerProvider { name: "fake" | "anthropic"; stream(req: AnswerRequest, signal: AbortSignal): AsyncGenerator<string, AnswerResult> }`
- `reserveBudget(db, day, amountNano, limitNano): Promise<boolean>`, `settleBudget(db, day, reservedNano, actualNano): Promise<void>`
- `handleAsk(request: Request, deps: AskDeps): Promise<Response>`
- NDJSON events: `meta`, `delta`, `done`, `error` (Task 24)

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement settings and the command skeleton**

`rag/src/ottie_rag/__init__.py`:

```python
"""Ask Ottie build, checks and evals."""
```

`rag/src/ottie_rag/settings.py`:

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import tomllib
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

RAG_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Paths:
    library: Path
    projects: Path
    writing: Path
    suggested: Path
    stopwords: Path
    denylist: Path
    index_out: Path
    query_embeddings: Path
    golden: Path
    adversarial: Path


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Chunking:
    max_chars: int
    min_chars: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Search:
    top_k: int
    rrf_k: int
    bm25_k1: float
    bm25_b: float
    dense_threshold: float
    bm25_threshold: float
    keyword_coverage: float


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EmbedderCfg:
    model: str
    revision: str
    pooling: str
    dim: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Settings:
    root: Path
    paths: Paths
    chunking: Chunking
    search: Search
    embedder: EmbedderCfg
    scan_globs: tuple[str, ...]
    source_file: Path


def load_settings(path: Path | None = None) -> Settings:
    path = (path or RAG_DIR / "rag.toml").resolve()
    raw = tomllib.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    p = dict(raw["paths"])
    root = (path.parent / p.pop("repo_root")).resolve()
    return Settings(
        root=root,
        paths=Paths(**{k: (root / v).resolve() for k, v in p.items()}),
        chunking=Chunking(**raw["chunking"]),
        search=Search(**raw["search"]),
        embedder=EmbedderCfg(**raw["embedder"]),
        scan_globs=tuple(raw["checks"]["scan_globs"]),
        source_file=path,
    )
```

`rag/src/ottie_rag/cli.py` (later tasks add subcommands to `_build_parser` and `_COMMANDS`):

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import sys
from collections.abc import Callable
from pathlib import Path

from .settings import Settings, load_settings


def _cmd_settings(args: argparse.Namespace, settings: Settings) -> int:
    print(f"root: {settings.root}")
    for name, value in vars(settings.paths).items():
        print(f"{name}: {value}")
    return 0


_COMMANDS: dict[str, Callable[[argparse.Namespace, Settings], int]] = {
    "settings": _cmd_settings,
}


def _build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="ottie-rag", description="Ask Ottie build, checks and evals")
    parser.add_argument("--settings", type=Path, default=None, help="path to rag.toml")
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    sub.add_parser("settings", help="print resolved paths")
    return parser


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    args = _build_parser().parse_args(argv)
    settings = load_settings(args.settings)
    return _COMMANDS[args.command](args, settings)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

### Task 2: Frontmatter and corpus loading

**Files:**
- Create: `rag/src/ottie_rag/frontmatter.py`, `rag/src/ottie_rag/corpus.py`
- Create fixtures: `rag/tests/fixtures/content/library/{approved-entry,unapproved-entry,sample-entry,french-entry}.md`, `rag/tests/fixtures/content/projects/{demo-project,draft-project}.mdx`, `rag/tests/fixtures/content/writing/demo-post.mdx`
- Test: `rag/tests/test_corpus.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Settings` (Task 1).
- Produces: `split_frontmatter(text: str) -> tuple[dict, str]`; `Document` (frozen dataclass: `source: str` repo-relative posix path, `url: str | None`, `kind: Literal["library","project","writing"]`, `title: str`, `project: str | None`, `language: Literal["en","fr"]`, `body: str`, `sample: bool`, `approved_on: date | None`); `LibraryFrontmatter` (pydantic); `LibraryFile(path: Path, source: str, meta: LibraryFrontmatter, body: str)`; `load_library_files(settings) -> list[LibraryFile]`; `load_documents(settings) -> list[Document]` (approved or sample library entries plus published projects and writing, sorted by `source`).

M1's frontmatter (from M1's interface note; M1's Zod schema stays the authority for the site build): library `topic`, `audience` (admissions, recruiters or visitors, at least one), `sources` as `[{label, url?}]`, `language`, `approved_on`, `sample`, plus an optional `project` slug that M3 asks M1 to add (Assumes); library files are `**/[^_]*.md`; URLs end in a slash (`/work/<id>/`); projects and writing `title`, `status: draft | published`, optional `language`; writing optional `project`.

```python
from datetime import date
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from ottie_rag.corpus import load_documents, load_library_files
from ottie_rag.frontmatter import split_frontmatter
from ottie_rag.settings import load_settings

SETTINGS = load_settings(Path(__file__).parent / "fixtures" / "rag.test.toml")


def test_split_frontmatter_returns_meta_and_body():
    meta, body = split_frontmatter("---\ntitle: A\nstatus: published\n---\n## H\n\nText\n")
    assert meta == {"title": "A", "status": "published"}
    assert body == "## H\n\nText\n"


def test_split_frontmatter_without_frontmatter():
    assert split_frontmatter("Just text") == ({}, "Just text")


def test_split_frontmatter_rejects_unclosed_block():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="unclosed frontmatter"):
        split_frontmatter("---\ntitle: A\nno end")


def test_documents_include_approved_samples_and_published_only():
    docs = load_documents(SETTINGS)
    sources = [d.source for d in docs]
    assert sources == sorted(sources)
    assert "content/library/approved-entry.md" in sources
    assert "content/library/sample-entry.md" in sources
    assert "content/library/french-entry.md" in sources
    assert "content/projects/demo-project.mdx" in sources
    assert "content/writing/demo-post.mdx" in sources
    assert "content/library/unapproved-entry.md" not in sources
    assert "content/projects/draft-project.mdx" not in sources


def test_document_fields():
    docs = {d.source: d for d in load_documents(SETTINGS)}
    lib = docs["content/library/approved-entry.md"]
    assert (lib.kind, lib.url, lib.project, lib.language) == ("library", None, "demo-project", "en")
    assert lib.title == "Fixture: what the demo pipeline does"
    assert lib.approved_on == date(2026, 11, 2)
    proj = docs["content/projects/demo-project.mdx"]
    assert (proj.kind, proj.url, proj.project, proj.title) == ("project", "/work/demo-project/", "demo-project", "Demo Project")
    post = docs["content/writing/demo-post.mdx"]
    assert (post.kind, post.url, post.project) == ("writing", "/writing/demo-post/", "demo-project")
    assert docs["content/library/sample-entry.md"].sample is True
    assert docs["content/library/french-entry.md"].language == "fr"


def test_library_files_include_unapproved_for_checks():
    names = [f.path.name for f in load_library_files(SETTINGS)]
    assert "unapproved-entry.md" in names

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement**

`rag/src/ottie_rag/frontmatter.py`:

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import yaml


def split_frontmatter(text: str) -> tuple[dict, str]:
    """Split a leading YAML block fenced by '---' lines from the body."""
    if not text.startswith("---\n"):
        return {}, text
    end = text.find("\n---\n", 4)
    if end == -1:
        if text.endswith("\n---"):
            end = len(text) - 4
        else:
            raise ValueError("unclosed frontmatter block")
    meta = yaml.safe_load(text[4:end]) or {}
    if not isinstance(meta, dict):
        raise ValueError("frontmatter must be a mapping")
    return meta, text[end + 5 :]
```

`rag/src/ottie_rag/corpus.py`:

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field

from .frontmatter import split_frontmatter
from .settings import Settings

Kind = Literal["library", "project", "writing"]
Language = Literal["en", "fr"]


class LibrarySource(BaseModel):
    label: str = Field(min_length=1)
    url: str | None = None


class LibraryFrontmatter(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")
    topic: str = Field(min_length=1)
    audience: list[Literal["admissions", "recruiters", "visitors"]] = Field(min_length=1)
    sources: list[LibrarySource] = Field(min_length=1)
    language: Language = "en"
    approved_on: date | None = None
    project: str | None = None
    sample: bool = False


class PageFrontmatter(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="ignore")
    title: str = Field(min_length=1)
    status: Literal["draft", "published"]
    language: Language = "en"
    project: str | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Document:
    source: str
    url: str | None
    kind: Kind
    title: str
    project: str | None
    language: Language
    body: str
    sample: bool
    approved_on: date | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LibraryFile:
    path: Path
    source: str
    meta: LibraryFrontmatter
    body: str


def _source(settings: Settings, path: Path) -> str:
    return path.resolve().relative_to(settings.root).as_posix()


def _files(folder: Path, *suffixes: str) -> list[Path]:
    if not folder.exists():
        return []
    return sorted(p for p in folder.iterdir() if p.is_file() and p.suffix in suffixes)


def load_library_files(settings: Settings) -> list[LibraryFile]:
    out = []
    folder = settings.paths.library
    paths = sorted(p for p in folder.rglob("*.md") if not p.name.startswith("_")) if folder.exists() else []
    for path in paths:
        meta, body = split_frontmatter(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        out.append(LibraryFile(path, _source(settings, path), LibraryFrontmatter.model_validate(meta), body))
    return out


def _pages(settings: Settings, folder: Path, kind: Kind, prefix: str) -> list[Document]:
    docs = []
    for path in _files(folder, ".md", ".mdx"):
        meta, body = split_frontmatter(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        fm = PageFrontmatter.model_validate(meta)
        if fm.status != "published":
            continue
        slug = path.stem
        project = slug if kind == "project" else fm.project
        docs.append(Document(_source(settings, path), f"{prefix}/{slug}/", kind, fm.title, project, fm.language, body, False, None))
    return docs


def load_documents(settings: Settings) -> list[Document]:
    docs = [
        Document(f.source, None, "library", f.meta.topic, f.meta.project, f.meta.language, f.body, f.meta.sample, f.meta.approved_on)
        for f in load_library_files(settings)
        if f.meta.approved_on is not None or f.meta.sample
    ]
    docs += _pages(settings, settings.paths.projects, "project", "/work")
    docs += _pages(settings, settings.paths.writing, "writing", "/writing")
    return sorted(docs, key=lambda d: d.source)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `textclean.py`**

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Section:
    heading: str
    text: str


_FENCE = re.compile(r"^\s*(```|~~~)")
_HEADING = re.compile(r"^(#{1,6})\s+(.+?)\s*#*\s*$")
_IMPORT_EXPORT = re.compile(r"^(import|export)\s")
_JSX_SELF = re.compile(r"<[A-Z][\w.]*\b[^>]*/>")
_JSX_TAG = re.compile(r"</?[A-Z][\w.]*\b[^>]*>")
_HTML_TAG = re.compile(r"</?[a-z][^>]*>")
_HTML_COMMENT = re.compile(r"<!--.*?-->", re.S)
_MDX_COMMENT = re.compile(r"\{/\*.*?\*/\}", re.S)
_IMAGE = re.compile(r"!\[([^\]]*)\]\([^)]*\)")
_LINK = re.compile(r"\[([^\]]+)\]\([^)]*\)")
_STRONG = re.compile(r"(\*\*|__)(.+?)\1")
_EM = re.compile(r"(?<![\w*_])[*_](\S(?:.*?\S)?)[*_](?![\w*_])")
_CODE = re.compile(r"`([^`]+)`")
_QUOTE = re.compile(r"^\s*>\s?")
_LIST = re.compile(r"^\s*(?:[-*+]|\d+[.)])\s+")
_SPACES = re.compile(r"\s+")


def clean_inline(text: str) -> str:
    text = _IMAGE.sub(r"\1", text)
    text = _LINK.sub(r"\1", text)
    text = _CODE.sub(r"\1", text)
    text = _STRONG.sub(r"\2", text)
    text = _EM.sub(r"\1", text)
    text = _HTML_TAG.sub("", text)
    return _SPACES.sub(" ", text).strip()


def mdx_to_sections(body: str) -> list[Section]:
    body = _MDX_COMMENT.sub("", _HTML_COMMENT.sub("", body))
    sections: list[Section] = []
    paras: list[str] = []
    buf: list[str] = []
    h2: str | None = None
    heading = ""
    in_fence = False

    def flush_para() -> None:
        if buf:
            paras.append(" ".join(buf))
            buf.clear()

    def flush_section() -> None:
        flush_para()
        if paras:
            sections.append(Section(heading, "\n\n".join(paras)))
            paras.clear()

    for raw in body.splitlines():
        if _FENCE.match(raw):
            in_fence = not in_fence
            flush_para()
            continue
        if in_fence or _IMPORT_EXPORT.match(raw):
            continue
        m = _HEADING.match(raw)
        if m:
            level, title = len(m.group(1)), clean_inline(m.group(2))
            if level == 1:
                flush_para()
                continue
            flush_section()
            if level == 2 or h2 is None:
                h2 = title
                heading = title
            else:
                heading = f"{h2} > {title}"
            continue
        line = _JSX_TAG.sub("", _JSX_SELF.sub("", raw))
        if not line.strip():
            flush_para()
            continue
        is_item = bool(_LIST.match(line))
        text = clean_inline(_LIST.sub("", _QUOTE.sub("", line)))
        if not text:
            continue
        if is_item:
            flush_para()
            buf.append(f"- {text}")
            flush_para()
        else:
            buf.append(text)
    flush_section()
    return sections
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `chunking.py`**

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import re
import unicodedata
from dataclasses import dataclass

from .corpus import Document
from .settings import Settings
from .textclean import Section, mdx_to_sections


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Chunk:
    id: str
    source: str
    url: str | None
    kind: str
    project: str | None
    language: str
    title: str
    heading: str
    text: str
    sample: bool


_SENTENCE = re.compile(r"(?<=[.!?])\s+")


def slugify(text: str) -> str:
    folded = "".join(c for c in unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", text.lower()) if not unicodedata.category(c).startswith("M"))
    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "-", folded).strip("-")


def _pack(parts: list[str], max_chars: int, joiner: str) -> list[str]:
    out: list[str] = []
    cur = ""
    for part in parts:
        if len(part) > max_chars:
            if cur:
                out.append(cur)
                cur = ""
            out.extend(part[i : i + max_chars] for i in range(0, len(part), max_chars))
            continue
        candidate = f"{cur}{joiner}{part}" if cur else part
        if len(candidate) <= max_chars:
            cur = candidate
        else:
            out.append(cur)
            cur = part
    if cur:
        out.append(cur)
    return out


def _split(text: str, max_chars: int) -> list[str]:
    if len(text) <= max_chars:
        return [text]
    pieces: list[str] = []
    for para in text.split("\n\n"):
        pieces.extend([para] if len(para) <= max_chars else _pack(_SENTENCE.split(para), max_chars, " "))
    return _pack(pieces, max_chars, "\n\n")


def _merge_small(sections: list[Section], min_chars: int) -> list[Section]:
    merged: list[Section] = []
    for s in sections:
        if merged and len(s.text) < min_chars:
            prev = merged[-1]
            label = f"{s.heading}: " if s.heading and s.heading != prev.heading else ""
            merged[-1] = Section(prev.heading, f"{prev.text}\n\n{label}{s.text}")
        else:
            merged.append(s)
    return merged


def chunk_document(doc: Document, max_chars: int, min_chars: int) -> list[Chunk]:
    chunks: list[Chunk] = []
    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    for s in _merge_small(mdx_to_sections(doc.body), min_chars):
        key = slugify(s.heading) or "intro"
        for piece in _split(s.text, max_chars):
            n = counts.get(key, 0)
            counts[key] = n + 1
            chunks.append(Chunk(f"{doc.source}#{key}-{n}", doc.source, doc.url, doc.kind, doc.project, doc.language, doc.title, s.heading, piece, doc.sample))
    return chunks


def chunk_corpus(docs: list[Document], settings: Settings) -> list[Chunk]:
    out: list[Chunk] = []
    for d in sorted(docs, key=lambda d: d.source):
        out.extend(chunk_document(d, settings.chunking.max_chars, settings.chunking.min_chars))
    return out


def chunk_index_text(chunk: Chunk) -> str:
    return "\n".join(part for part in (chunk.title, chunk.heading, chunk.text) if part)
```

### Task 4: Keyword tokenizer, term hash and the shared text fixture

The Worker must tokenize exactly like the build, or keyword search silently breaks. Both sides read one stopword file and are tested against one committed fixture.

**Files:**
- Create: `site/src/lib/ask/stopwords.json`
- Create: `rag/src/ottie_rag/textnorm.py`, `rag/src/ottie_rag/fixtures.py`
- Create (generated, committed): `rag/tests/fixtures/shared/text-cases.json`
- Modify: `rag/src/ottie_rag/cli.py` (add `fixtures`)
- Test: `rag/tests/test_textnorm.py`, `rag/tests/test_fixtures_current.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `STOPWORDS: frozenset[str]`; `tokenize(text: str, keep_stopwords: bool = False) -> list[str]`; `fnv1a32(s: str) -> int`; `normalize_question(q: str) -> str`; `question_key(q: str) -> bytes` (sha256 of the UTF-8 normalized question); `fixtures.regenerate(out_dir: Path) -> dict[str, bytes]`; `fixtures.TEXT_CASES: list[str]`.
- The tokenizer rule (both languages): lowercase, NFKD, drop every code point whose Unicode category starts with `M`, take runs of `[a-z0-9]`, keep tokens of length 2 or more, drop stopwords unless `keep_stopwords`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `textnorm.py`**

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import re
import unicodedata
from functools import cache
from pathlib import Path

_STOPWORDS_FILE = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3] / "site/src/lib/ask/stopwords.json"
_WORD = re.compile(r"[a-z0-9]+")
_SPACES = re.compile(r"\s+")


@cache
def _stopwords() -> frozenset[str]:
    return frozenset(json.loads(_STOPWORDS_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["words"])


def fold(text: str) -> str:
    decomposed = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", text.lower())
    return "".join(c for c in decomposed if not unicodedata.category(c).startswith("M"))


def tokenize(text: str, keep_stopwords: bool = False) -> list[str]:
    stop = _stopwords()
    return [w for w in _WORD.findall(fold(text)) if len(w) >= 2 and (keep_stopwords or w not in stop)]


def fnv1a32(s: str) -> int:
    h = 0x811C9DC5
    for b in s.encode("utf-8"):
        h ^= b
        h = (h * 0x01000193) & 0xFFFFFFFF
    return h


def normalize_question(q: str) -> str:
    return _SPACES.sub(" ", unicodedata.normalize("NFC", q)).strip()


def question_key(q: str) -> bytes:
    return hashlib.sha256(normalize_question(q).encode("utf-8")).digest()
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `fixtures.py` (the text part; Tasks 9 and 10 add more files)**

```python
"""Regenerate fixtures that both pytest and Vitest read. Never edit the outputs by hand."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import Callable
from pathlib import Path

from .textnorm import fnv1a32, normalize_question, question_key, tokenize

TEXT_CASES = [
    "What is Ask Ottie?",
    "Quels sont les résultats du projet ?",
    "How old is Din?",
    "Qu'est-ce que c'est ? 🙂",
    "H-1B and bge-m3 in 2026",
    "Ça coûte combien, l'hébergement ?",
    "cœur, ÆSIR, straße, İstanbul",
    "  spaced\tout question  ",
    "Pipeline stages: load, validate, publish.",
    "",
]


def _json(data: object) -> bytes:
    return (json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n").encode("utf-8")


def _text_cases() -> bytes:
    cases = []
    for text in TEXT_CASES:
        tokens = tokenize(text)
        cases.append({
            "text": text,
            "tokens": tokens,
            "tokens_all": tokenize(text, keep_stopwords=True),
            "fnv1a32": [fnv1a32(t) for t in tokens],
            "normalized": normalize_question(text),
            "question_key": question_key(text).hex(),
        })
    return _json({"version": 1, "cases": cases})


GENERATORS: dict[str, Callable[[], bytes]] = {
    "text-cases.json": _text_cases,
}


def regenerate(out_dir: Path) -> dict[str, bytes]:
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    produced = {}
    for name, gen in GENERATORS.items():
        data = gen()
        (out_dir / name).write_bytes(data)
        produced[name] = data
    return produced
```

def test_postings_are_sorted_by_hash_and_chunk():

Expected: FAIL with `ModuleNotFoundError`

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from collections import Counter
from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np

from .textnorm import fnv1a32


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class KeywordIndex:
    term_hashes: np.ndarray
    starts: np.ndarray
    post_ids: np.ndarray
    post_tf: np.ndarray
    doc_len: np.ndarray
    avgdl: float


def build_keyword_index(token_lists: list[list[str]]) -> KeywordIndex:
    postings: dict[int, dict[int, int]] = {}
    owner: dict[int, str] = {}
    for doc_id, tokens in enumerate(token_lists):
        for term, tf in Counter(tokens).items():
            h = fnv1a32(term)
            first = owner.setdefault(h, term)
            if first != term:
                raise ValueError(f"term hash collision: {first!r} and {term!r}")
            postings.setdefault(h, {})[doc_id] = min(tf, 65535)
    hashes = sorted(postings)
    starts, ids, tfs = [0], [], []
    for h in hashes:
        for doc_id in sorted(postings[h]):
            ids.append(doc_id)
            tfs.append(postings[h][doc_id])
        starts.append(len(ids))
    doc_len = np.array([len(t) for t in token_lists], dtype=np.uint32)
    return KeywordIndex(
        term_hashes=np.array(hashes, dtype=np.uint32),
        starts=np.array(starts, dtype=np.uint32),
        post_ids=np.array(ids, dtype=np.uint32),
        post_tf=np.array(tfs, dtype=np.uint16),
        doc_len=doc_len,
        avgdl=float(doc_len.mean()) if len(token_lists) else 0.0,
    )
```

**Interfaces:**

import numpy as np

from ottie_rag.embedders import HashEmbedder, make_embedder, quantize_int8
from ottie_rag.settings import EmbedderCfg


def test_hash_embedder_is_deterministic_and_normalized():
    e = HashEmbedder(dim=16)
    a = e.embed(["pipeline stages", "results"])
    b = e.embed(["pipeline stages", "results"])
    assert a.dtype == np.float32 and a.shape == (2, 16)
    assert np.array_equal(a, b)
    assert np.allclose(np.linalg.norm(a, axis=1), 1.0, atol=1e-6)
    assert e.id == "hash-v1:16"


def test_hash_embedder_shared_words_are_closer():
    e = HashEmbedder(dim=64)
    q, near, far = e.embed(["pipeline validation", "the pipeline validation stage", "otter glasses"])
    assert float(q @ near) > float(q @ far)


def test_hash_embedder_empty_text_is_a_zero_row():
    assert not HashEmbedder(dim=8).embed(["?"]).any()


def test_make_embedder_hash():
    assert make_embedder(EmbedderCfg("hash", "v1", "cls", 16)).id == "hash-v1:16"


def test_int8_quantization_keeps_cosine():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    v = rng.standard_normal((50, 1024)).astype(np.float32)
    v /= np.linalg.norm(v, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    q, scales = quantize_int8(v)
    assert q.dtype == np.int8 and scales.dtype == np.float32
    restored = q.astype(np.float32) * scales[:, None]
    cos = np.sum(restored * v, axis=1) / np.linalg.norm(restored, axis=1)
    assert float(cos.min()) >= 0.999
    assert int(np.abs(q).max()) == 127


def test_int8_quantization_zero_vector():
    q, scales = quantize_int8(np.zeros((1, 4), np.float32))
    assert not q.any() and scales[0] == 1.0

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from typing import Protocol

import numpy as np

from .settings import EmbedderCfg
from .textnorm import tokenize


class Embedder(Protocol):
    id: str
    dim: int

    def embed(self, texts: list[str]) -> np.ndarray: ...


def _token_vector(token: str, dim: int) -> np.ndarray:
    values: list[float] = []
    counter = 0
    while len(values) < dim:
        digest = hashlib.sha256(f"{token}:{counter}".encode()).digest()
        counter += 1
        values.extend(int.from_bytes(digest[j : j + 4], "little") / 0xFFFFFFFF * 2 - 1 for j in range(0, 32, 4))
    return np.array(values[:dim], dtype=np.float64)


class HashEmbedder:
    """Deterministic bag-of-words vectors for tests. Not a semantic model."""

    def __init__(self, dim: int = 16) -> None:
        self.dim = dim
        self.id = f"hash-v1:{dim}"

    def embed(self, texts: list[str]) -> np.ndarray:
        out = np.zeros((len(texts), self.dim), dtype=np.float32)
        for i, text in enumerate(texts):
            v = np.zeros(self.dim, dtype=np.float64)
            for token in tokenize(text):
                v += _token_vector(token, self.dim)
            norm = float(np.linalg.norm(v))
            if norm > 0:
                out[i] = (v / norm).astype(np.float32)
        return out


class BgeM3Embedder:
    """bge-m3 dense embeddings from the open weights, CPU only."""

    FILES = [
        "config.json",
        "pytorch_model.bin",
        "tokenizer.json",
        "tokenizer_config.json",
        "special_tokens_map.json",
        "sentencepiece.bpe.model",
        "modules.json",
        "sentence_bert_config.json",
        "config_sentence_transformers.json",
        "1_Pooling/config.json",
    ]

    def __init__(self, cfg: EmbedderCfg) -> None:
        from huggingface_hub import snapshot_download
        from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer

        path = snapshot_download(cfg.model, revision=cfg.revision, allow_patterns=self.FILES)
        self.model = SentenceTransformer(path, device="cpu")
        if cfg.pooling == "mean":
            from sentence_transformers.models import Pooling

            self.model._modules["1"] = Pooling(cfg.dim, pooling_mode="mean")
        elif cfg.pooling != "cls":
            raise ValueError(f"unknown pooling {cfg.pooling!r}")
        self.model.max_seq_length = 1024
        self.dim = cfg.dim
        self.id = f"{cfg.model}@{cfg.revision}:{cfg.pooling}"

    def embed(self, texts: list[str]) -> np.ndarray:
        vectors = self.model.encode(texts, batch_size=8, normalize_embeddings=True, convert_to_numpy=True, show_progress_bar=len(texts) > 32)
        return np.asarray(vectors, dtype=np.float32)


def make_embedder(cfg: EmbedderCfg) -> Embedder:
    if cfg.model == "hash":
        return HashEmbedder(cfg.dim)
    return BgeM3Embedder(cfg)


def quantize_int8(vectors: np.ndarray) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    v = np.asarray(vectors, dtype=np.float32)
    if v.size == 0:
        return v.astype(np.int8), np.zeros(len(v), dtype=np.float32)
    peak = np.abs(v).max(axis=1)
    scales = np.where(peak > 0, peak / 127.0, 1.0).astype(np.float32)
    q = np.clip(np.rint(v / scales[:, None]), -127, 127).astype(np.int8)
    return q, scales
```

The `allow_patterns` list keeps the download to about 2.29 GB: without it, a snapshot also fetches the ONNX copy (`onnx/model.onnx_data`, another 2.27 GB) and `colbert_linear.pt`.

### Task 7: The binary index format (v1) and the query-embedding cache

**Files:**
- Create: `rag/src/ottie_rag/index_format.py`, `rag/INDEX_FORMAT.md`
- Test: `rag/tests/test_index_format.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `KeywordIndex` (Task 5), `question_key` (Task 4).
- Produces: `MAGIC = b"OTIX"`, `VERSION = 1`, `HEADER_BYTES = 96`, `RECORD_BYTES = 24`, `KINDS = ("library","project","writing")`, `LANGS = ("en","fr")`, `FLAG_SAMPLES = 1`; `IndexParams(avgdl, dense_threshold, bm25_threshold, keyword_coverage, rrf_k, bm25_k1, bm25_b)`; `ChunkRecord(text, title, heading, url, source, project, kind, language, doc_len)`; `IndexData(version, dim, flags, corpus_hash, params, vectors, scales, records, projects, term_hashes, starts, post_ids, post_tf)` with `.count`; `layout(n, d, t, p) -> dict[str, int]`; `write_index(*, vectors, scales, records, keyword, params, corpus_hash, flags) -> bytes`; `read_index(data: bytes) -> IndexData`; `rewrite_params(data: bytes, params: IndexParams) -> bytes` (rewrites the threshold fields only); `IndexFormatError`; `write_query_cache(entries: dict[bytes, np.ndarray], dim: int) -> bytes`; `read_query_cache(data: bytes) -> QueryCache` with `.dim`, `.keys() -> list[bytes]`, `.get(question: str) -> np.ndarray | None`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the byte layout document**

`rag/INDEX_FORMAT.md`:

````markdown
# Ask Ottie index format, version 1

`site/public/ask/index-v1.bin` is written by `rag/` (`ottie_rag.index_format.write_index`) and read by the Worker (`site/src/lib/ask/index-format.ts`). Everything is little-endian. A reader must reject a file whose magic, version or total length does not match.

## Header (96 bytes)

| Offset | Size | Type | Field |
|---:|---:|---|---|
| 0 | 4 | ASCII | magic `OTIX` |
| 4 | 2 | u16 | format version, `1` |
| 6 | 2 | u16 | vector dimension `D` |
| 8 | 4 | u32 | chunk count `N` |
| 12 | 4 | u32 | term count `T` |
| 16 | 4 | u32 | posting count `P` |
| 20 | 4 | u32 | string table bytes `S` |
| 24 | 4 | u32 | flags (bit 0: the index contains sample entries) |
| 28 | 4 | f32 | BM25 average document length |
| 32 | 4 | f32 | dense threshold (don't-know gate, hybrid mode) |
| 36 | 4 | f32 | BM25 threshold (don't-know gate, keyword mode) |
| 40 | 4 | f32 | minimum query-term coverage (keyword mode) |
| 44 | 4 | u32 | reciprocal rank fusion constant `k` |
| 48 | 4 | f32 | BM25 `k1` |
| 52 | 4 | f32 | BM25 `b` |
| 56 | 4 | u32 | project list offset in the string table |
| 60 | 4 | u32 | project list length in bytes |
| 64 | 32 | bytes | sha256 of the corpus (chunks, chunking settings, embedder id) |

## Sections

Offsets follow from the counts, so there is no offset table. `align4(x)` rounds up to a multiple of 4.

| Section | Offset | Content |
|---|---|---|
| vectors | 96 | `N × D` int8, row-major. Chunk vectors are L2-normalized before quantization. |
| scales | `align4(96 + N·D)` | `N` f32. Cosine of chunk `i` with a unit query `q` is `scale[i] · Σ vector[i][k] · q[k]`. |
| records | scales + `4N` | `N` records of 24 bytes (below). |
| term hashes | records + `24N` | `T` u32 FNV-1a hashes of terms, ascending. |
| term starts | term hashes + `4T` | `T + 1` u32: postings of term `t` are `[start[t], start[t+1])`. |
| posting chunk ids | term starts + `4(T+1)` | `P` u32 chunk indexes, ascending within each term. |
| posting term frequencies | posting ids + `4P` | `P` u16. |
| strings | `align4(tf + 2P)` | `S` bytes of UTF-8. Total file length is `strings + S`. |

Record (24 bytes): `u32 text_off`, `u32 text_len`, `u32 meta_off`, `u32 meta_len`, `u16 project_id` (0 = none, otherwise 1 + the index in the project list), `u8 kind` (0 library, 1 project, 2 writing), `u8 language` (0 en, 1 fr), `u32 doc_len` (BM25 token count). The meta string is `title`, `heading`, `url` (empty when none) and `source`, separated by U+001F. The project list is project slugs separated by U+001F, sorted.

## Search algorithm v1 (both readers implement it the same way)

1. Candidates: all chunks, or only chunks whose project is the requested project. An unknown project has no candidates.
2. Query terms: the unique tokens of the retrieval query (tokenizer in `site/src/lib/ask/text.ts` and `rag/src/ottie_rag/textnorm.py`), sorted.
3. BM25 per candidate with `idf = ln(1 + (N - df + 0.5) / (df + 0.5))` and the header's `k1`, `b`, average length.
4. Hybrid mode (a query vector is present): dense cosine as above. Rank candidates by dense (top 50) and by BM25 among chunks with a score above 0 (top 50). Fused score `Σ 1 / (k + rank)`. Order by fused, then dense, then chunk index. Answerable when the best dense score among candidates is at least the dense threshold.
5. Keyword mode (no vector, for example Workers AI is down): order by BM25 among chunks with a score above 0, then chunk index. Answerable when the best chunk's BM25 is at least the BM25 threshold and it matches at least the coverage fraction of the query terms.
6. Return the top 6.

## Query-embedding cache (`rag/evals/query-embeddings.bin`)

| Offset | Size | Type | Field |
|---:|---:|---|---|
| 0 | 4 | ASCII | magic `OTQE` |
| 4 | 2 | u16 | version `1` |
| 6 | 2 | u16 | dimension `D` |
| 8 | 4 | u32 | count `C` |
| 12 | 4 | u32 | reserved, 0 |
| 16 | `C × (32 + 4D)` | | records sorted by key: 32-byte sha256 of the normalized retrieval query, then `D` f32 |

The retrieval query is the question, or the previous question, a newline and the question for follow-ups.
````

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing tests**

`rag/tests/test_index_format.py`:

```python
import struct

import numpy as np
import pytest

from ottie_rag.index_format import (
    HEADER_BYTES,
    ChunkRecord,
    IndexFormatError,
    IndexParams,
    layout,
    read_index,
    read_query_cache,
    rewrite_params,
    write_index,
    write_query_cache,
)
from ottie_rag.keyword import build_keyword_index
from ottie_rag.textnorm import question_key, tokenize

PARAMS = IndexParams(avgdl=0.0, dense_threshold=0.45, bm25_threshold=2.0, keyword_coverage=0.5, rrf_k=60, bm25_k1=1.2, bm25_b=0.75)


def sample_bytes(flags: int = 0) -> bytes:
    texts = ["Pipeline stages: load and validate.", "Les résultats sont publiés 🙂", "Ottie wears glasses."]
    tokens = [tokenize(t) for t in texts]
    kw = build_keyword_index(tokens)
    records = [
        ChunkRecord(texts[0], "Demo", "Stages", "/work/demo", "content/projects/demo.mdx", "demo", "project", "en", len(tokens[0])),
        ChunkRecord(texts[1], "Résultats", "", None, "content/library/fr.md", "demo", "library", "fr", len(tokens[1])),
        ChunkRecord(texts[2], "Ottie", "", None, "content/library/ottie.md", None, "library", "en", len(tokens[2])),
    ]
    rng = np.random.default_rng(1)
    vectors = rng.integers(-127, 128, size=(3, 8), dtype=np.int8)
    scales = np.array([0.01, 0.02, 0.03], dtype=np.float32)
    params = IndexParams(**{**PARAMS.__dict__, "avgdl": kw.avgdl})
    return write_index(vectors=vectors, scales=scales, records=records, keyword=kw, params=params, corpus_hash=bytes(range(32)), flags=flags)


def test_round_trip():
    data = sample_bytes(flags=1)
    ix = read_index(data)
    assert (ix.version, ix.dim, ix.count, ix.flags) == (1, 8, 3, 1)
    assert ix.corpus_hash == bytes(range(32))
    assert ix.projects == ["demo"]
    assert ix.records[1].text == "Les résultats sont publiés 🙂"
    assert ix.records[1].title == "Résultats" and ix.records[1].url is None and ix.records[1].language == "fr"
    assert ix.records[0].url == "/work/demo" and ix.records[0].project == "demo" and ix.records[0].kind == "project"
    assert ix.records[2].project is None
    assert ix.params.dense_threshold == pytest.approx(0.45) and ix.params.rrf_k == 60
    assert ix.scales.dtype == np.float32 and ix.vectors.shape == (3, 8)


def test_sections_are_aligned_and_length_is_exact():
    data = sample_bytes()
    n, d, t, p, s = struct.unpack_from("<IIII", data, 8)[0], struct.unpack_from("<H", data, 6)[0], *struct.unpack_from("<III", data, 12)
    offsets = layout(n, d, t, p)
    assert all(v % 4 == 0 for k, v in offsets.items() if k != "vectors")
    assert offsets["vectors"] == HEADER_BYTES
    assert len(data) == offsets["strings"] + s


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "mutate, message",
    [
        (lambda b: b"XXXX" + b[4:], "magic"),
        (lambda b: b[:4] + struct.pack("<H", 2) + b[6:], "version 2"),
        (lambda b: b[:-1], "length"),
        (lambda b: b[:50], "too short"),
    ],
)
def test_bad_files_are_rejected(mutate, message):
    with pytest.raises(IndexFormatError, match=message):
        read_index(mutate(sample_bytes()))


def test_rewrite_params_changes_only_thresholds():
    data = sample_bytes()
    new = rewrite_params(data, IndexParams(**{**read_index(data).params.__dict__, "dense_threshold": 0.5}))
    assert len(new) == len(data)
    assert read_index(new).params.dense_threshold == pytest.approx(0.5)
    assert new[96:] == data[96:]


def test_query_cache_round_trip():
    entries = {question_key("What is Ask Ottie?"): np.ones(4, np.float32), question_key("Qui est Ottie ?"): np.arange(4, dtype=np.float32)}
    cache = read_query_cache(write_query_cache(entries, dim=4))
    assert cache.dim == 4
    assert cache.keys() == sorted(entries)
    assert np.array_equal(cache.get(" What is  Ask Ottie? "), np.ones(4, np.float32))
    assert cache.get("unknown") is None
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `index_format.py`**

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import struct
from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np

from .keyword import KeywordIndex
from .textnorm import question_key

MAGIC = b"OTIX"
VERSION = 1
HEADER_BYTES = 96
RECORD_BYTES = 24
KINDS = ("library", "project", "writing")
LANGS = ("en", "fr")
FLAG_SAMPLES = 1
SEP = "\x1f"
_HEADER = struct.Struct("<4sHHIIIIIffffIffII32s")
_RECORD = struct.Struct("<IIIIHBBI")
_PARAMS_AT = 32  # dense_threshold, bm25_threshold, keyword_coverage (3 x f32) start here
assert _HEADER.size == HEADER_BYTES and _RECORD.size == RECORD_BYTES

QMAGIC = b"OTQE"
_QHEADER = struct.Struct("<4sHHII")


class IndexFormatError(ValueError):
    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class IndexParams:
    avgdl: float
    dense_threshold: float
    bm25_threshold: float
    keyword_coverage: float
    rrf_k: int
    bm25_k1: float
    bm25_b: float


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ChunkRecord:
    text: str
    title: str
    heading: str
    url: str | None
    source: str
    project: str | None
    kind: str
    language: str
    doc_len: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class IndexData:
    version: int
    dim: int
    flags: int
    corpus_hash: bytes
    params: IndexParams
    vectors: np.ndarray
    scales: np.ndarray
    records: list[ChunkRecord]
    projects: list[str]
    term_hashes: np.ndarray
    starts: np.ndarray
    post_ids: np.ndarray
    post_tf: np.ndarray

    @property
    def count(self) -> int:
        return len(self.records)


def _align4(n: int) -> int:
    return (n + 3) & ~3


def layout(n: int, d: int, t: int, p: int) -> dict[str, int]:
    o = {"vectors": HEADER_BYTES}
    o["scales"] = _align4(o["vectors"] + n * d)
    o["records"] = o["scales"] + 4 * n
    o["term_hashes"] = o["records"] + RECORD_BYTES * n
    o["starts"] = o["term_hashes"] + 4 * t
    o["post_ids"] = o["starts"] + 4 * (t + 1)
    o["post_tf"] = o["post_ids"] + 4 * p
    o["strings"] = _align4(o["post_tf"] + 2 * p)
    return o


def write_index(*, vectors: np.ndarray, scales: np.ndarray, records: list[ChunkRecord], keyword: KeywordIndex, params: IndexParams, corpus_hash: bytes, flags: int) -> bytes:
    n = len(records)
    d = int(vectors.shape[1]) if vectors.ndim == 2 else 0
    if vectors.shape[0] != n or len(scales) != n:
        raise ValueError("vectors, scales and records must have the same length")
    if len(corpus_hash) != 32:
        raise ValueError("corpus_hash must be 32 bytes")
    t, p = len(keyword.term_hashes), len(keyword.post_ids)
    projects = sorted({r.project for r in records if r.project})
    strings = bytearray()

    def put(s: str) -> tuple[int, int]:
        b = s.encode("utf-8")
        off = len(strings)
        strings.extend(b)
        return off, len(b)

    proj_off, proj_len = put(SEP.join(projects))
    recs = bytearray()
    for r in records:
        text_off, text_len = put(r.text)
        meta_off, meta_len = put(SEP.join([r.title, r.heading, r.url or "", r.source]))
        pid = projects.index(r.project) + 1 if r.project else 0
        recs += _RECORD.pack(text_off, text_len, meta_off, meta_len, pid, KINDS.index(r.kind), LANGS.index(r.language), r.doc_len)

    o = layout(n, d, t, p)
    buf = bytearray(o["strings"] + len(strings))
    _HEADER.pack_into(
        buf, 0, MAGIC, VERSION, d, n, t, p, len(strings), flags,
        params.avgdl, params.dense_threshold, params.bm25_threshold, params.keyword_coverage,
        params.rrf_k, params.bm25_k1, params.bm25_b, proj_off, proj_len, corpus_hash,
    )
    buf[o["vectors"] : o["vectors"] + n * d] = np.ascontiguousarray(vectors, dtype=np.int8).tobytes()
    buf[o["scales"] : o["scales"] + 4 * n] = np.asarray(scales, dtype="<f4").tobytes()
    buf[o["records"] : o["records"] + len(recs)] = recs
    buf[o["term_hashes"] : o["starts"]] = np.asarray(keyword.term_hashes, dtype="<u4").tobytes()
    buf[o["starts"] : o["post_ids"]] = np.asarray(keyword.starts, dtype="<u4").tobytes()
    buf[o["post_ids"] : o["post_tf"]] = np.asarray(keyword.post_ids, dtype="<u4").tobytes()
    buf[o["post_tf"] : o["post_tf"] + 2 * p] = np.asarray(keyword.post_tf, dtype="<u2").tobytes()
    buf[o["strings"] :] = strings
    return bytes(buf)


def read_index(data: bytes) -> IndexData:
    if len(data) < HEADER_BYTES:
        raise IndexFormatError("index too short")
    (magic, version, d, n, t, p, s, flags, avgdl, dense_t, bm25_t, cov, rrf_k, k1, b, proj_off, proj_len, chash) = _HEADER.unpack_from(data, 0)
    if magic != MAGIC:
        raise IndexFormatError("bad magic")
    if version != VERSION:
        raise IndexFormatError(f"unsupported index version {version}")
    o = layout(n, d, t, p)
    if len(data) != o["strings"] + s:
        raise IndexFormatError(f"index length {len(data)} does not match layout {o['strings'] + s}")
    strings = data[o["strings"] :]

    def text(off: int, length: int) -> str:
        return strings[off : off + length].decode("utf-8")

    projects = text(proj_off, proj_len).split(SEP) if proj_len else []
    records = []
    for i in range(n):
        to, tl, mo, ml, pid, kind, lang, dl = _RECORD.unpack_from(data, o["records"] + i * RECORD_BYTES)
        title, heading, url, source = text(mo, ml).split(SEP)
        records.append(ChunkRecord(text(to, tl), title, heading, url or None, source, projects[pid - 1] if pid else None, KINDS[kind], LANGS[lang], dl))
    return IndexData(
        version=version, dim=d, flags=flags, corpus_hash=chash,
        params=IndexParams(avgdl, dense_t, bm25_t, cov, rrf_k, k1, b),
        vectors=np.frombuffer(data, dtype=np.int8, count=n * d, offset=o["vectors"]).reshape(n, d),
        scales=np.frombuffer(data, dtype="<f4", count=n, offset=o["scales"]),
        records=records, projects=projects,
        term_hashes=np.frombuffer(data, dtype="<u4", count=t, offset=o["term_hashes"]),
        starts=np.frombuffer(data, dtype="<u4", count=t + 1, offset=o["starts"]),
        post_ids=np.frombuffer(data, dtype="<u4", count=p, offset=o["post_ids"]),
        post_tf=np.frombuffer(data, dtype="<u2", count=p, offset=o["post_tf"]),
    )


def rewrite_params(data: bytes, params: IndexParams) -> bytes:
    read_index(data)
    buf = bytearray(data)
    struct.pack_into("<fff", buf, _PARAMS_AT, params.dense_threshold, params.bm25_threshold, params.keyword_coverage)
    struct.pack_into("<I", buf, 44, params.rrf_k)
    struct.pack_into("<ff", buf, 48, params.bm25_k1, params.bm25_b)
    return bytes(buf)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class QueryCache:
    dim: int
    entries: dict[bytes, np.ndarray]

    def keys(self) -> list[bytes]:
        return sorted(self.entries)

    def get(self, question: str) -> np.ndarray | None:
        return self.entries.get(question_key(question))


def write_query_cache(entries: dict[bytes, np.ndarray], dim: int) -> bytes:
    out = bytearray(_QHEADER.pack(QMAGIC, 1, dim, len(entries), 0))
    for key in sorted(entries):
        vec = np.asarray(entries[key], dtype="<f4")
        if len(key) != 32 or vec.shape != (dim,):
            raise ValueError("bad query cache entry")
        out += key + vec.tobytes()
    return bytes(out)


def read_query_cache(data: bytes) -> QueryCache:
    magic, version, dim, count, _ = _QHEADER.unpack_from(data, 0)
    if magic != QMAGIC or version != 1:
        raise IndexFormatError("bad query cache header")
    size = 32 + 4 * dim
    if len(data) != _QHEADER.size + count * size:
        raise IndexFormatError("query cache length mismatch")
    entries = {}
    for i in range(count):
        at = _QHEADER.size + i * size
        entries[data[at : at + 32]] = np.frombuffer(data, dtype="<f4", count=dim, offset=at + 32).copy()
    return QueryCache(dim, entries)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np

from .index_format import IndexData
from .textnorm import fnv1a32, tokenize

TOP_K = 6
POOL = 50


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Hit:
    chunk: int
    dense: float | None
    bm25: float
    matched: int
    fused: float


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SearchResult:
    mode: str
    hits: list[Hit]
    answerable: bool
    top_dense: float | None
    query_terms: int


def _bm25(ix: IndexData, terms: list[str]) -> tuple[list[float], list[int]]:
    n = ix.count
    scores, matched = [0.0] * n, [0] * n
    p = ix.params
    if n == 0 or p.avgdl <= 0:
        return scores, matched
    for term in terms:
        h = fnv1a32(term)
        pos = int(np.searchsorted(ix.term_hashes, h))
        if pos >= len(ix.term_hashes) or int(ix.term_hashes[pos]) != h:
            continue
        start, end = int(ix.starts[pos]), int(ix.starts[pos + 1])
        df = end - start
        idf = math.log(1 + (n - df + 0.5) / (df + 0.5))
        for j in range(start, end):
            d = int(ix.post_ids[j])
            tf = int(ix.post_tf[j])
            dl = ix.records[d].doc_len
            scores[d] += idf * tf * (p.bm25_k1 + 1) / (tf + p.bm25_k1 * (1 - p.bm25_b + p.bm25_b * dl / p.avgdl))
            matched[d] += 1
    return scores, matched


def search(ix: IndexData, qvec: np.ndarray | None, question: str, project: str | None = None, top_k: int = TOP_K) -> SearchResult:
    if project is None:
        cand = list(range(ix.count))
    else:
        cand = [i for i, r in enumerate(ix.records) if r.project == project]
    terms = sorted(set(tokenize(question)))
    bm25, matched = _bm25(ix, terms)

    if qvec is None:
        ranked = sorted((c for c in cand if bm25[c] > 0), key=lambda c: (-bm25[c], c))[:top_k]
        hits = [Hit(c, None, bm25[c], matched[c], bm25[c]) for c in ranked]
        best = hits[0] if hits else None
        answerable = bool(best) and best.bm25 >= ix.params.bm25_threshold and best.matched / max(len(terms), 1) >= ix.params.keyword_coverage
        return SearchResult("keyword", hits, answerable, None, len(terms))

    q = np.asarray(qvec, dtype=np.float32).astype(np.float64)
    if q.shape != (ix.dim,):
        raise ValueError(f"query vector has {q.shape[0]} dims, index has {ix.dim}")
    norm = float(np.sqrt(np.sum(q * q)))
    if norm > 0:
        q = q / norm
    dense = {c: float(ix.vectors[c].astype(np.float64) @ q) * float(ix.scales[c]) for c in cand}
    fused = {c: 0.0 for c in cand}
    for rank, c in enumerate(sorted(cand, key=lambda c: (-dense[c], c))[:POOL], start=1):
        fused[c] += 1.0 / (ix.params.rrf_k + rank)
    for rank, c in enumerate(sorted((c for c in cand if bm25[c] > 0), key=lambda c: (-bm25[c], c))[:POOL], start=1):
        fused[c] += 1.0 / (ix.params.rrf_k + rank)
    ranked = sorted(cand, key=lambda c: (-fused[c], -dense[c], c))[:top_k]
    hits = [Hit(c, dense[c], bm25[c], matched[c], fused[c]) for c in ranked]
    top_dense = max(dense.values()) if dense else None
    answerable = top_dense is not None and top_dense >= ix.params.dense_threshold
    return SearchResult("hybrid", hits, answerable, top_dense, len(terms))
```

```python
import shutil

import pytest
from helpers import FIXTURES, build_fixture_index, fixture_settings

from ottie_rag.build import build_index_bytes, collect_chunks, corpus_hash, verify_fresh
from ottie_rag.embedders import HashEmbedder
from ottie_rag.index_format import FLAG_SAMPLES, read_index
from ottie_rag.settings import load_settings


def test_build_contains_every_chunk_and_flags_samples():
    ix, _, settings = build_fixture_index()
    chunks = collect_chunks(settings)
    assert ix.count == len(chunks) > 0
    assert [r.source for r in ix.records] == [c.source for c in chunks]
    assert ix.flags & FLAG_SAMPLES
    assert ix.projects == ["demo-project"]
    assert ix.params.dense_threshold == pytest.approx(0.2)


def test_build_is_deterministic():
    settings = fixture_settings()
    assert build_index_bytes(settings, HashEmbedder(16)) == build_index_bytes(settings, HashEmbedder(16))


def test_hash_ignores_thresholds_but_not_text(tmp_path):
    settings = fixture_settings()
    chunks = collect_chunks(settings)
    h = corpus_hash(chunks, settings, "hash-v1:16")
    edited = [chunks[0].__class__(**{**chunks[0].__dict__, "text": chunks[0].text + " changed"})] + chunks[1:]
    assert corpus_hash(edited, settings, "hash-v1:16") != h
    assert corpus_hash(chunks, settings, "other-embedder") != h


def test_verify_fresh_reports_missing_stale_and_param_drift(tmp_path):
    root = tmp_path / "fx"
    shutil.copytree(FIXTURES, root, ignore=shutil.ignore_patterns("shared", "out"))
    toml = root / "rag.test.toml"
    toml.write_text(toml.read_text().replace("../../../site", str(FIXTURES.parents[2] / "site")))
    settings = load_settings(toml)
    assert "missing" in verify_fresh(settings)[0]

    settings.paths.index_out.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
    settings.paths.index_out.write_bytes(build_index_bytes(settings, HashEmbedder(16)))
    assert verify_fresh(settings) == []

    (root / "content/library/approved-entry.md").write_text((root / "content/library/approved-entry.md").read_text() + "\nMore text.\n")
    assert any("stale" in p for p in verify_fresh(settings))

    settings.paths.index_out.write_bytes(build_index_bytes(settings, HashEmbedder(16)))
    toml.write_text(toml.read_text().replace("dense_threshold = 0.2", "dense_threshold = 0.3"))
    assert any("set-params" in p for p in verify_fresh(load_settings(toml)))

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from dataclasses import asdict

import numpy as np

from .chunking import Chunk, chunk_corpus, chunk_index_text
from .corpus import load_documents
from .embedders import Embedder, quantize_int8
from .index_format import FLAG_SAMPLES, ChunkRecord, IndexParams, read_index, write_index
from .keyword import build_keyword_index
from .settings import Settings
from .textnorm import tokenize

REBUILD_HINT = "Run `pnpm --dir site ask:index` on Din's machine and commit site/public/ask/index-v1.bin and rag/evals/query-embeddings.bin."


def collect_chunks(settings: Settings) -> list[Chunk]:
    return chunk_corpus(load_documents(settings), settings)


def expected_embedder_id(settings: Settings) -> str:
    e = settings.embedder
    return f"hash-v1:{e.dim}" if e.model == "hash" else f"{e.model}@{e.revision}:{e.pooling}"


def corpus_hash(chunks: list[Chunk], settings: Settings, embedder_id: str) -> bytes:
    payload = {
        "format": 1,
        "embedder": embedder_id,
        "chunking": asdict(settings.chunking),
        "chunks": [asdict(c) for c in chunks],
    }
    return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")).digest()


def params_from_settings(settings: Settings, avgdl: float) -> IndexParams:
    s = settings.search
    return IndexParams(avgdl, s.dense_threshold, s.bm25_threshold, s.keyword_coverage, s.rrf_k, s.bm25_k1, s.bm25_b)


def build_index_bytes(settings: Settings, embedder: Embedder) -> bytes:
    chunks = collect_chunks(settings)
    texts = [chunk_index_text(c) for c in chunks]
    vectors = embedder.embed(texts) if texts else np.zeros((0, embedder.dim), np.float32)
    q, scales = quantize_int8(vectors)
    tokens = [tokenize(t) for t in texts]
    kw = build_keyword_index(tokens)
    records = [ChunkRecord(c.text, c.title, c.heading, c.url, c.source, c.project, c.kind, c.language, len(tok)) for c, tok in zip(chunks, tokens)]
    return write_index(
        vectors=q, scales=scales, records=records, keyword=kw,
        params=params_from_settings(settings, kw.avgdl),
        corpus_hash=corpus_hash(chunks, settings, embedder.id),
        flags=FLAG_SAMPLES if any(c.sample for c in chunks) else 0,
    )


def _f32(x: float) -> float:
    return float(np.float32(x))


def verify_fresh(settings: Settings) -> list[str]:
    path = settings.paths.index_out
    if not path.exists():
        return [f"{path} is missing. {REBUILD_HINT}"]
    ix = read_index(path.read_bytes())
    problems = []
    if ix.corpus_hash != corpus_hash(collect_chunks(settings), settings, expected_embedder_id(settings)):
        problems.append(f"the index is stale: the library, the published pages or the chunking settings changed. {REBUILD_HINT}")
    if ix.dim != settings.embedder.dim:
        problems.append(f"the index has {ix.dim} dims, rag.toml says {settings.embedder.dim}. {REBUILD_HINT}")
    s, p = settings.search, ix.params
    if (_f32(s.dense_threshold), _f32(s.bm25_threshold), _f32(s.keyword_coverage), s.rrf_k, _f32(s.bm25_k1), _f32(s.bm25_b)) != (
        p.dense_threshold, p.bm25_threshold, p.keyword_coverage, p.rrf_k, p.bm25_k1, p.bm25_b,
    ):
        problems.append("the search parameters in rag.toml differ from the index header. Run `uv run --project rag ottie-rag set-params` and commit the index.")
    return problems
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Add the CLI commands**

In `cli.py`, add subparsers `build` (with `--embedder` choices `configured` and `hash`, default `configured`, and `--out` path), `set-params`, `verify-fresh`, and:

```python
def _cmd_build(args: argparse.Namespace, settings: Settings) -> int:
    from .build import build_index_bytes
    from .embedders import HashEmbedder, make_embedder

    embedder = HashEmbedder(settings.embedder.dim) if args.embedder == "hash" else make_embedder(settings.embedder)
    data = build_index_bytes(settings, embedder)
    out = args.out or settings.paths.index_out
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out.write_bytes(data)
    print(f"wrote {out} ({len(data):,} bytes, embedder {embedder.id})")
    return 0


def _cmd_set_params(args: argparse.Namespace, settings: Settings) -> int:
    from .build import params_from_settings
    from .index_format import read_index, rewrite_params

    data = settings.paths.index_out.read_bytes()
    ix = read_index(data)
    settings.paths.index_out.write_bytes(rewrite_params(data, params_from_settings(settings, ix.params.avgdl)))
    print(f"updated search parameters in {settings.paths.index_out}")
    return 0


def _cmd_verify_fresh(args: argparse.Namespace, settings: Settings) -> int:
    from .build import verify_fresh

    problems = verify_fresh(settings)
    for p in problems:
        print(f"error: {p}", file=sys.stderr)
    if not problems:
        print("index is fresh")
    return 1 if problems else 0
```

Register `"build"`, `"set-params"`, `"verify-fresh"` in `_COMMANDS`.

```python
import json as _json_mod

from .build import build_index_bytes
from .embedders import HashEmbedder
from .index_format import read_index, write_query_cache
from .search import search
from .settings import load_settings
from .textnorm import question_key

_FIXTURE_SETTINGS = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "tests" / "fixtures" / "rag.test.toml"

PARITY_QUERIES: list[tuple[str, str | None, bool]] = [
    ("What does the demo pipeline do?", None, True),
    ("pipeline stages", "demo-project", True),
    ("Quels sont les résultats du pipeline ?", None, True),
    ("validation bad rows", None, False),
    ("🙂 ?", None, True),
    ("zebra quantum", None, False),
    ("Who wrote the notes?", "unknown-project", True),
]


def _parity() -> tuple[bytes, bytes, bytes]:
    settings = load_settings(_FIXTURE_SETTINGS)
    embedder = HashEmbedder(settings.embedder.dim)
    data = build_index_bytes(settings, embedder)
    ix = read_index(data)
    cases, cache = [], {}
    for question, project, use_dense in PARITY_QUERIES:
        qvec = embedder.embed([question])[0] if use_dense else None
        if qvec is not None:
            cache[question_key(question)] = qvec
        r = search(ix, qvec, question, project)
        cases.append({
            "question": question,
            "project": project,
            "qvec": [float(x) for x in qvec] if qvec is not None else None,
            "expected": {
                "mode": r.mode, "answerable": r.answerable, "top_dense": r.top_dense, "query_terms": r.query_terms,
                "hits": [{"chunk": h.chunk, "dense": h.dense, "bm25": h.bm25, "matched": h.matched, "fused": h.fused} for h in r.hits],
            },
        })
    chunks = [{"source": rec.source, "title": rec.title, "heading": rec.heading, "url": rec.url, "project": rec.project, "kind": rec.kind, "language": rec.language, "text": rec.text} for rec in ix.records]
    return data, _json({"version": 1, "chunks": chunks, "cases": cases}), write_query_cache(cache, settings.embedder.dim)


def _parity_index() -> bytes:
    return _parity()[0]


def _parity_cases() -> bytes:
    return _parity()[1]


def _parity_queries() -> bytes:
    return _parity()[2]


GENERATORS.update({
    "parity-index.bin": _parity_index,
    "parity-cases.json": _parity_cases,
    "parity-queries.bin": _parity_queries,
})
```

(Put the imports at the top of the module with the others; `GENERATORS.update` goes after `GENERATORS` is defined.)

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the shared deny-list**

`site/src/lib/ask/denylist.json`. Terms are matched as whole token phrases (accents folded, stopwords kept), so "address" in "to address the problem" never matches "home address". Single words that are common in project write-ups (bank, health, partner, sponsor) are only listed inside phrases.

```json
{
  "version": 1,
  "topics": [
    {
      "id": "immigration",
      "label": "visa and immigration status",
      "terms": ["visa", "visas", "immigration", "immigrant", "work permit", "green card", "h1b", "h 1b", "stem opt", "opt extension", "sponsorship", "visa sponsor", "citizenship", "permanent resident", "residency status", "titre de sejour", "permis de travail", "statut d immigration", "parrainage"]
    },

      "terms": ["salary", "salaries", "income", "net worth", "debt", "debts", "student loan", "student loans", "bank account", "savings", "how much money", "how much does din earn", "tuition fees", "salaire", "revenu", "revenus", "dette", "dettes", "pret etudiant", "combien gagne"]
    },
    {

```

Allow rules, added only with Din's approval, look like `{"source": "site/src/content/projects/x.mdx", "term": "bank account", "reason": "the project is about bank account fraud detection", "approved_on": "2026-11-20"}`.

Note: `"marie"` matches the folded form of "marié" and also the name Marie. If a real page names a Marie, Din adds an allow rule for that file.

import shutil
from datetime import date

from helpers import FIXTURES

def test_denylist_matches_phrases_in_both_languages():
    assert [h.topic for h in match_denylist(DL, "Is Din on a work visa?")] == ["immigration"]
    assert [h.topic for h in match_denylist(DL, "What salary is Din looking for?")] == ["finances"]
    assert [h.topic for h in match_denylist(DL, "How old is Din?")] == ["personal"]
    assert [h.topic for h in match_denylist(DL, "Din a-t-il besoin d'un visa ?")] == ["immigration"]

    assert [h.topic for h in match_denylist(DL, "Quel âge a Din ?")] == ["personal"]

    page = root / "content/writing/demo-post.mdx"
    page.write_text(page.read_text() + "\nI moved here on a student visa.\n")
    problems = run_checks(settings, today=date(2026, 11, 20))

    problems = run_checks(settings, release=True, today=date(2026, 11, 20))

def test_allow_rule_skips_one_term_in_one_file(tmp_path):

    assert not any("demo-post.mdx" in p for p in run_checks(load_settings(toml), today=date(2026, 11, 20)))

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `denylist.py`**

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

from .textnorm import tokenize


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DenyTopic:
    id: str
    label: str
    phrases: tuple[tuple[str, ...], ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AllowRule:
    source: str
    term: str
    reason: str
    approved_on: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DenyList:
    topics: tuple[DenyTopic, ...]
    allow: tuple[AllowRule, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DenyHit:
    topic: str
    term: str


def load_denylist(path: Path) -> DenyList:
    raw = json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    topics = tuple(
        DenyTopic(t["id"], t["label"], tuple(p for p in (tuple(tokenize(term, keep_stopwords=True)) for term in t["terms"]) if p))
        for t in raw["topics"]
    )
    allow = tuple(AllowRule(a["source"], " ".join(tokenize(a["term"], keep_stopwords=True)), a["reason"], a["approved_on"]) for a in raw.get("allow", []))
    return DenyList(topics, allow)


def _contains(tokens: list[str], phrase: tuple[str, ...]) -> bool:
    n = len(phrase)
    return any(tuple(tokens[i : i + n]) == phrase for i in range(len(tokens) - n + 1))


def match_denylist(dl: DenyList, text: str) -> list[DenyHit]:
    tokens = tokenize(text, keep_stopwords=True)
    hits = []
    for topic in dl.topics:
        for phrase in topic.phrases:
            if _contains(tokens, phrase):
                hits.append(DenyHit(topic.id, " ".join(phrase)))
    return hits
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `checks.py`**

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal

import yaml
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, ValidationError

from .corpus import load_library_files
from .denylist import load_denylist, match_denylist
from .frontmatter import split_frontmatter
from .index_format import FLAG_SAMPLES, read_index
from .settings import Settings
from .textnorm import tokenize


class SuggestedSource(BaseModel):
    label: str = Field(min_length=1)
    href: str = Field(min_length=1)


class SuggestedEntry(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    id: str = Field(pattern=r"^[a-z0-9-]+$")
    question: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=120)
    answer: str = Field(default="", max_length=1500)
    sources: list[SuggestedSource] = []
    language: Literal["en", "fr"] = "en"
    order: int = Field(ge=1)
    generated_on: date | None = None
    status: Literal["draft", "published"] = "draft"


def suggested_file(settings: Settings) -> Path:
    return settings.paths.suggested / "suggested.yaml"


def load_suggested(settings: Settings) -> list[SuggestedEntry]:
    path = suggested_file(settings)
    if not path.exists():
        return []
    return [SuggestedEntry.model_validate(x) for x in (yaml.safe_load(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) or [])]


def _rel(settings: Settings, path: Path) -> str:
    return path.resolve().relative_to(settings.root).as_posix()


def _first_line(text: str, phrase: str) -> int:
    want = phrase.split()
    for number, line in enumerate(text.splitlines(), start=1):
        tokens = tokenize(line, keep_stopwords=True)
        if any(tokens[i : i + len(want)] == want for i in range(len(tokens) - len(want) + 1)):
            return number
    return 1


def _is_draft(path: Path, text: str) -> bool:
    if path.suffix not in (".md", ".mdx"):
        return False
    try:
        meta, _ = split_frontmatter(text)
    except ValueError:
        return False
    return meta.get("status") == "draft"


def run_checks(settings: Settings, release: bool = False, today: date | None = None) -> list[str]:
    today = today or date.today()
    problems: list[str] = []
    library_paths = {p.resolve() for p in settings.paths.library.glob("*.md")} if settings.paths.library.exists() else set()

    try:
        library = load_library_files(settings)
    except ValidationError as err:
        return [f"library frontmatter is invalid: {err}"]
    for f in library:
        if f.meta.approved_on is None and not f.meta.sample:
            problems.append(f"{f.source}: approved_on is missing. Only Din sets it, after reviewing the entry in a PR.")
        if f.meta.approved_on and f.meta.approved_on > today:
            problems.append(f"{f.source}: approved_on {f.meta.approved_on} is in the future.")
        if release and f.meta.sample:
            problems.append(f"{f.source}: sample entries cannot ship in a release. Remove it, or make it a real entry Din approves.")

    rel_suggested = _rel(settings, suggested_file(settings)) if suggested_file(settings).exists() else ""
    for entry in load_suggested(settings):
        if entry.status == "published" and (not entry.answer.strip() or not entry.sources or entry.generated_on is None):
            problems.append(f"{rel_suggested}: {entry.id} is published without an answer, sources and generated_on. Run `pnpm --dir site ask:suggested`, then Din reviews it.")

    dl = load_denylist(settings.paths.denylist)
    allowed = {(a.source, a.term) for a in dl.allow}
    seen: set[Path] = set()
    for pattern in settings.scan_globs:
        for path in sorted(settings.root.glob(pattern)):
            if path in seen or not path.is_file():
                continue
            seen.add(path)
            text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            if path.resolve() not in library_paths and _is_draft(path, text):
                continue
            rel = _rel(settings, path)
            for hit in match_denylist(dl, text):
                if (rel, hit.term) in allowed:
                    continue
                problems.append(f"{rel}:{_first_line(text, hit.term)}: mentions a deny-list topic ({hit.topic}: '{hit.term}'). Remove it, or ask Din to add an allow rule in site/src/lib/ask/denylist.json.")

    if release and settings.paths.index_out.exists() and read_index(settings.paths.index_out.read_bytes()).flags & FLAG_SAMPLES:
        problems.append(f"{_rel(settings, settings.paths.index_out)}: the index contains sample entries; rebuild it after removing them.")
    return problems
```

Add `check` to the CLI (`--release` flag):

```python
def _cmd_check(args: argparse.Namespace, settings: Settings) -> int:
    from .checks import run_checks

    problems = run_checks(settings, release=args.release)
    for p in problems:
        print(f"error: {p}", file=sys.stderr)
    print(f"{len(problems)} problem(s)" if problems else "content checks passed")
    return 1 if problems else 0
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the sample library entries**

Each file uses this frontmatter shape (only `topic`, `language` and `sources` change):

```markdown
---
topic: "Sample: what Ask Ottie is"
audience: [recruiters, admissions, visitors]
sources: [{label: "Site PRD, section 3 (docs/site/PRD.md)"}]
language: en
approved_on: null
sample: true
---
```

Bodies:

| File | topic | Body |
|---|---|---|
| `sample-what-is-ask-ottie.md` | Sample: what Ask Ottie is | Ask Ottie is the question box on dins.dev. Visitors ask about Din's work, projects, skills and education, and Ottie answers from a small library of entries that Din has approved, plus the published project and writing pages. Ottie keeps answers short and shows the sources each answer came from. |
| `sample-how-search-works.md` | Sample: how Ask Ottie finds sources | Each question is turned into a vector with the bge-m3 embedding model on Cloudflare Workers AI. Ottie then searches a prebuilt index in two ways at once, by meaning (cosine similarity) and by keywords (BM25), and merges the two rankings. The six best passages go to the answer model. On a project page, the search only looks at that project's sources. |
| `sample-dont-know.md` | Sample: when Ottie does not know | If no passage in the index is close enough to the question, Ottie says it doesn't know and points to Din's contact details. In that case the answer model is never called, so Ottie cannot guess or make something up. |
| `sample-answer-model.md` | Sample: which model writes the answers | Answers are written by Claude Haiku from Anthropic, using only the passages the search found. Answers are capped at about 350 tokens, the last three turns of a conversation are kept, and the instructions are cached so each answer stays cheap. |
| `sample-budget.md` | Sample: how Ask Ottie limits spending | Ask Ottie has a daily budget of about one US dollar, checked before every answer. When the budget is spent or the answer service is down, Ottie rests: it shows the pages that best match the question and Din's contact links instead of an answer. |
| `sample-privacy.md` | Sample: what Ask Ottie keeps | Ask Ottie keeps an anonymous log of questions for 30 days: the question, the sources used, their scores and any thumbs up or down. It stores no IP address and sets no cookies. The log helps Din find questions Ottie should answer better. Older entries are deleted every night. |
| `sample-who-is-ottie.md` | Sample: who Ottie is | Ottie is a cheerful brown otter with big round black glasses, the mascot and guide of dins.dev. Ottie greets visitors, explains, reacts and admits not knowing. Ottie always talks about Din and never speaks as Din. |
| `sample-site-stack.md` | Sample: how dins.dev is built | dins.dev is built with Astro, with Svelte for the interactive parts, and runs on Cloudflare Workers. Pages are plain HTML first; only Ask Ottie, Ottie and a few animated sections load JavaScript. The search index is built in Python and shipped as one binary file. |
| `sample-contact.md` | Sample: how to contact Din | The best way to reach Din is the contact page at /contact or an email to din@dins.dev. Ottie points visitors there whenever a question is something only Din can answer. |
| `sample-brand.md` | Sample: the dins brand | dins is Din's brand, always written in lowercase. The tagline is "Look closer.", a nod to Ottie's glasses and to attention to detail. The brand colour is a deep teal, and the type uses Fraunces, Instrument Sans and JetBrains Mono. |
| `sample-fr-ask-ottie.md` (`language: fr`) | Sample: ce qu'est Ask Ottie | Ask Ottie est la zone de questions de dins.dev. Les visiteurs posent des questions sur le travail, les projets et la formation de Din, et Ottie répond à partir d'une petite bibliothèque de textes approuvés par Din et des pages publiées du site. Ottie répond en français quand la question est en français et montre toujours ses sources. |
| `sample-fr-who-is-ottie.md` (`language: fr`) | Sample: qui est Ottie | Ottie est une loutre brune et joyeuse avec de grandes lunettes rondes noires, la mascotte et le guide de dins.dev. Ottie parle toujours de Din, jamais à la place de Din. |

`sources` labels per file: `Site PRD, section 3 (docs/site/PRD.md)` for the Ask Ottie entries, `Brand guide, Ottie (docs/BRAND.md)` for the Ottie entries, `Brand guide (docs/BRAND.md)` for the brand entry, `Site PRD, section 5 (docs/site/PRD.md)` for the stack entry, `Site PRD, section 2 (docs/site/PRD.md)` for the contact entry.
